Mathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCategory.Triangulated

CochainComplex.MappingConeCompHomotopyEquiv.homdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCategory.Triangulated, function or constant
Wording
the homomorphism of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$. Then the homomorphism of $f$ and $g$ is a homomorphism of the mapping cone of $g$ and the mapping cone of the first morphism of the mapping cone composition triangle of $f$ and $g$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{2} , x_{3}), g \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{3} , x_{4})\vdash \operatorname{hom} (f , g)\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{mappingCone} (g), \operatorname{mappingCone} (\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Pretriangulated\_\_Triangle\_\_mor\_u2081\_} (\operatorname{mappingConeCompTriangle} (f , g))))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$. Alors l'homomorphisme de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme du cône d'applications de $g$ et le cône d'applications du morphisme premier du triangle de compositions de cônes d'applications de $f$ et $g$.
CochainComplex.MappingConeCompHomotopyEquiv.homotopyInvHomIddefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCategory.Triangulated, function or constant
Wording
the homotopy inverse homomorphism identity of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$. Then the homotopy inverse homomorphism identity of $f$ and $g$ is a homotopy of the composition of the inverse of $f$ and $g$ and the homomorphism of $f$ and $g$ and the identity morphism of the mapping cone of the first morphism of the mapping cone composition triangle of $f$ and $g$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{2} , x_{3}), g \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{3} , x_{4})\vdash \operatorname{homotopyInvHomId} (f , g)\in \mathrm{Homotopy} (\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{inv} (f , g), \operatorname{hom} (f , g)), \operatorname{id} (\operatorname{mappingCone} (\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Pretriangulated\_\_Triangle\_\_mor\_u2081\_} (\operatorname{mappingConeCompTriangle} (f , g)))))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$. Alors l'identité d'homomorphismes inverse d'homotopies de $f$ et $g$ est une homotopie de la composition de l'inverse de $f$ et $g$ et l'homomorphisme de $f$ et $g$ et le morphisme d'identités du cône d'applications du morphisme premier du triangle de compositions de cônes d'applications de $f$ et $g$.
CochainComplex.MappingConeCompHomotopyEquiv.invdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCategory.Triangulated, function or constant
Wording
the inverse of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$. Then the inverse of $f$ and $g$ is a homomorphism of the mapping cone of the first morphism of the mapping cone composition triangle of $f$ and $g$ and the mapping cone of $g$.
Formula

CochainComplex.mappingConeCompHomotopyEquivdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCategory.Triangulated, function or constant
Wording
the mapping cone composition homotopy equivalence of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$. Then the mapping cone composition homotopy equivalence of $f$ and $g$ is a homotopy equivalence of the mapping cone of $g$ and the mapping cone of the first morphism of the mapping cone composition triangle of $f$ and $g$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{2} , x_{3}), g \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{3} , x_{4})\vdash \operatorname{mappingConeCompHomotopyEquiv} (f , g)\in \mathrm{HomotopyEquiv} (\operatorname{mappingCone} (g), \operatorname{mappingCone} (\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Pretriangulated\_\_Triangle\_\_mor\_u2081\_} (\operatorname{mappingConeCompTriangle} (f , g))))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$. Alors l'équivalence d'homotopies de compositions de cônes d'applications de $f$ et $g$ est une équivalence d'homotopies du cône d'applications de $g$ et le cône d'applications du morphisme premier du triangle de compositions de cônes d'applications de $f$ et $g$.
CochainComplex.mappingConeCompTriangledefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCategory.Triangulated, function or constant
Wording
the mapping cone composition triangle of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$. Then the mapping cone composition triangle of $f$ and $g$ is a triangle of the set of cochain complexes of $C$ and $\Integer$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{2} , x_{3}), g \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{3} , x_{4})\vdash \operatorname{mappingConeCompTriangle} (f , g)\in \operatorname{Triangle} (\mathrm{CochainComplex} (C , \Integer))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$. Alors le triangle de compositions de cônes d'applications de $f$ et $g$ est un triangle de l'ensemble de nombres complexes de cochaînes de $C$ et $\Integer$.
CochainComplex.mappingConeCompTrianglehdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCategory.Triangulated, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$. Then $\operatorname{mappingConeCompTriangleh}$ applied to $f$ and $g$ is a triangle of the set of homotopy categories of $C$ and $\operatorname{up}$ applied to $\Integer$ defined as the action of the map triangle of the quotient of $C$ and $\operatorname{up}$ applied to $\Integer$ and the mapping cone composition triangle of $f$ and $g$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{2} , x_{3}), g \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{3} , x_{4})\vdash \operatorname{mappingConeCompTriangleh} (f , g):= \operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{mapTriangle} (\operatorname{quotient} (C , \operatorname{up} (\Integer))), \operatorname{mappingConeCompTriangle} (f , g))\in \operatorname{Triangle} (\mathrm{HomotopyCategory} (C , \operatorname{up} (\Integer)))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$. Alors $\operatorname{mappingConeCompTriangleh}$ appliqué à $f$ et $g$ est un triangle de l'ensemble de catégories d'homotopies de $C$ et $\operatorname{up}$ appliqué à $\Integer$ défini comme l'action du triangle d'applications du quotient de $C$ et $\operatorname{up}$ appliqué à $\Integer$ et le triangle de compositions de cônes d'applications de $f$ et $g$.

Mathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCofiber

HomologicalComplex.HasCylinderdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCofiber, predicate
Wording
K has cylinders (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $c$. Then $K$ has cylinders, if the lift of the identity morphism of $K$ and $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to the identity morphism of $K$ has homotopy cofibers.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , c)\vdash \operatorname{HasCylinder} (K):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{HasHomotopyCofiber} (\operatorname{lift} (\operatorname{id} (K), \operatorname{neg} (\operatorname{id} (K))))$
French
Soit $K$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $c$. Alors $K$ a des cylindres si le lift du morphisme d'identités de $K$ et $\operatorname{neg}$ appliqué à le morphisme d'identités de $K$ a des cofibres d'homotopies.
HomologicalComplex.HasHomotopyCofiberclassMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCofiber, predicate
Wording
x3 has homotopy cofibers (from the name)
English
Let $x_{3}$ be a homomorphism of $F$ and $G$. Then we can say that $x_{3}$ has homotopy cofibers.
Formula
$x_{3} \in \operatorname{Hom} (F , G)\vdash \operatorname{HasHomotopyCofiber} (x_{3})\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $x_{3}$ un homomorphisme de $F$ et $G$. Alors nous pouvons dire que $x_{3}$ a des cofibres d'homotopies.
HomologicalComplex.cylinderdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCofiber, function or constant
Wording
the cylinder object of K (from the docstring)
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $c$. Then the cylinder object of $K$ is a homological complex of $C$ and $c$ defined as the homotopy cofiber of the lift of the identity morphism of $K$ and $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to the identity morphism of $K$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , c)\vdash \operatorname{cylinder} (K):= \operatorname{homotopyCofiber} (\operatorname{lift} (\operatorname{id} (K), \operatorname{neg} (\operatorname{id} (K))))\in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , c)$
French
Soit $K$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $c$. Alors l'objet de cylindres de $K$ est un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $c$ défini comme la cofibre d'homotopies du lift du morphisme d'identités de $K$ et $\operatorname{neg}$ appliqué à le morphisme d'identités de $K$.
HomologicalComplex.cylinder.descdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCofiber, function or constant
Wording
the induced morphism of x5 and h2 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $x_{4}$ and $x_{5}$ be homomorphisms of $K$ and $F$. Let $h_{2}$ be a homotopy of $x_{4}$ and $x_{5}$. Then the induced morphism of $x_{5}$ and $h_{2}$ is a homomorphism of the cylinder object of $K$ and $F$.
Formula
$x_{4} , x_{5} \in \operatorname{Hom} (K , F), h_{2} \in \mathrm{Homotopy} (x_{4} , x_{5})\vdash \operatorname{desc} (x_{4} , x_{5} , h_{2})\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{cylinder} (K), F)$
French
Soient $x_{4}$ et $x_{5}$ des homomorphismes de $K$ et $F$. Soit $h_{2}$ une homotopie de $x_{4}$ et $x_{5}$. Alors le morphisme induit de $x_{5}$ et $h_{2}$ est un homomorphisme de l'objet de cylindres de $K$ et $F$.
HomologicalComplex.cylinder.homotopyEquivdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCofiber, function or constant
Wording
the homotopy equivalence of K and hc (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $c$. Assume that for all elements $j$ of $x_{2}$, there exists an element $i$ of $x_{2}$, such that $\operatorname{Rel}$ holds for $c$, $i$ and $j$. Formula

Alors l'équivalence d'homotopies de $K$ et $\mathrm{hc}$ est une équivalence d'homotopies de l'objet de cylindres de $K$ et $K$.

Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $c$. Assume that for all elements $j$ of $x_{2}$, there exists an element $i$ of $x_{2}$, such that $\operatorname{Rel}$ holds for $c$, $i$ and $j$. HomologicalComplex.cylinder.inlXdefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCofiber, function or constant
Wording
the left injection of i and j (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $c$. Let $i$ and $j$ be elements of $x_{2}$ such that $\operatorname{Rel}$ holds for $c$, $j$ and $i$. Then the left injection of $i$ and $j$ is a homomorphism of $\operatorname{X}$ applied to $K$ and $i$ and $\operatorname{X}$ applied to the cylinder object of $K$ and $j$ defined as the left injection of $i$ and $j$.

HomologicalComplex.cylinder.mapHomologicalComplexObjIsodefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.HomotopyCofiber, function or constant
Wording
the map homological complex object isomorphism of F and H (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $c$. Let $H$ be a functor of $C$ and $D$. Assume that for all elements $j$ of $x_{2}$, there exists an element $i$ of $x_{2}$, such that $\operatorname{Rel}$ holds for $c$, $i$ and $j$. Then the map homological complex object isomorphism of $F$ and $H$ is an isomorphism of the action of the map homological complex of $H$ and $c$ and the cylinder object of $F$ and the cylinder object of the action of the map homological complex of $H$ and $c$ and $F$.
Formula
$F \in \mathrm{HomologicalComplex} (C , c), H \in \operatorname{Functor} (C , D), \forall j \in x_{2} . \exists i \in x_{2} . \operatorname{Rel} (c , i , j)\vdash \operatorname{mapHomologicalComplexObjIso} (F , H , \mathrm{hc})\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{mapHomologicalComplex} (H , c), \operatorname{cylinder} (F)), \operatorname{cylinder} (\operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{mapHomologicalComplex} (H , c), F)))$
French
Soit $F$ un nombre complexe homologique de $C$ et $c$. Soit $H$ un foncteur de $C$ et $D$. Supposons que pour tous les éléments $j$ de $x_{2}$, il existe un élément $i$ de $x_{2}$ tel que $\operatorname{Rel}$ tienne pour $c$, $i$ et $j$. Alors l'isomorphisme d'objets complexe homologique d'applications de $F$ et $H$ est un isomorphisme de l'action du nombre complexe homologique d'applications de $H$ et $c$ et l'objet de cylindres de $F$ et l'objet de cylindres de l'action du nombre complexe homologique d'applications de $H$ et $c$ et $F$.

English
Let $K$ be a homological complex of $C$ and $c$. Then the left inclusion of $K$ is a homomorphism of $K$ and the cylinder object of $K$ defined as the composition of the left injection and the right inclusion of the lift of the identity morphism of $K$ and $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to the identity morphism of $K$.

French

the null homotopic map of K (from the name)
English

Then the null homotopic map of $K$ is a homomorphism of the cylinder object of $K$ and the cylinder object of $K$ defined as the null homotopic map of the function that maps $i$, $j$ and $\mathrm{hij}$ to the composition of the second projection of the lift of the identity morphism of $K$ and $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to the identity morphism of $K$ and $i$ and the composition of $\operatorname{f}$ applied to the projection onto the second summand and $i$ and the left injection of $i$ and $j$.

Soit $C$ un ensemble. Soit $c$ une forme complexe de $x_{2}$. Soient $i$, $j$ et $k$ des éléments de $x_{2}$. Alors le foncteur complexe court de $j$ et $k$ est un foncteur de l'ensemble de nombres complexes homologiques de $C$ et $c$ et l'ensemble de nombres complexes courts de $C$.
